  For a function F(p), dF/dθ_j = ∑_i ∂F/∂p_i * (p_i * (cos/sin term - mean over i of p_i*term)). Because dp_i/dθ = p_i (t_i - mean_p t_i) (where mean weighted p). This is like covariance under p.

def linear_conv_p_a(p,a): # p length L=2n-1, a length n
    n=len(a); L=len(p); size=1 << (L+n-2).bit_length()
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,size); Ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],size); return np.fft.irfft(P*Ar,size)[:L+n-1]
Then grad = (2/beta)*out[n-1:n-1+n].

def smooth_vals(a, beta, offset=None):
  c=autocorr(a)
  if offset is None: offset=c.max()
  # use only top K? all to avoid switching, but if beta high exp of many low negligible; still compute vector size 2n.
  shifted=beta*(c-offset); shifted=np.clip(shifted,-700,0)
  e=np.exp(shifted); s=e.sum(); if s<1: p=onehot... else p=e/s
  return offset + (1/beta)*np.log(s), p, c

Generate basis at full/proxy. Let's write top-level:
```
def make_basis(n):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    xm=x-0.5
    cols=[xm, xm*xm, xm*xm*xm, xm**4]
    for m in range(1,5):
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x))
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x))
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    # maybe subtract each column mean? Not necessary; but to reduce flat? Constant not included. xm^2 has mean .25. Fine.
    return B
```

Detailed function implementations:

```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

`autocorr_fft(a)`:
```
a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
n = a.size
m = 2*n -1
size = next_pow2(m)
A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)
return c[:m]
```
Maybe return `np.maximum(c, 0.0)`? For LSE, tiny negative may affect offset? Offset max positive. If all c negative due bad? no. We can not clip to preserve exact max. But numerical negative values could make logsumexp include tiny positive? c max positive. OK.

`conv_weights_a(w,a)`:
```
n=a.size; m=w.size
size=next_pow2(m+n-1)
W=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
U=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1].copy(), size)
out=np.fft.irfft(W*U,size)
return out[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Need ensure a[::-1] contiguous maybe copy to avoid? rfft can handle. `a[::-1]` creates view negative stride; rfft may copy; fine.

`smooth_lse(a,beta)`:
```
c=autocorr_fft(a)
offset=float(c.max())
shifted=beta*(c-offset)
# clip lower to -745, upper 0
np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0, out=shifted) # shifted writable? if c default
e=np.exp(shifted)
s=float(e.sum())
if s <= 0.0:
   p=np.zeros_like(c); im=int(np.argmax(c)); p[im]=1.0
else:
   p=e/s

```

return B
```

def full_b_fun(x, data):
   n=data['n']; beta=data['beta']
   y=np.empty(n); y[0]=0.0; y[1:]=x
   z=y-y.max(); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return inf
   a=e/s
   f0,pw,c=smooth_lse(a,beta)
   F=n*f0
   if np.isfinite(F) and F < data['best_F']:
       data['best_F']=float(F); data['best_x']=x.copy()
   return float(F)
```

- Compute c and top_idx = argsort(c)[-10:]
- pressure = sum over top peaks: for valid idx, add a[k-idx] to idx and a[idx] to k-idx. This gives involvement.
- Select source = argmax pressure (maybe top few)
- Destination = argmin pressure among those not in top peak regions and not source.
- Try fractions [0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03] and update if score improves (using FFT).

def local_peak_move(a0, time_limit, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_c = autocorr_fft(a).max(); best_score=2*n*best_c
    t0=...
    no_improve=0
    max_stall=60
    while time < limit and no_improve < max_stall:
       c = autocorr_fft(a)
       cmax = c.max()
       if cmax < best_c - 1e-12: best...
       else: no_improve +=1
       L=len(c); n=len(a)
       top = np.argsort(c)[-12:]
       pressure=np.zeros(n)
       in_peak=np.zeros(n,bool)
       for k in top:
          ki=int(k); s=max(0,ki-n+1); e=min(n-1,ki)
          if s<=e:
             idx=np.arange(s,e+1)
             part=a[ki-idx]
             pressure[idx]+=part
             pressure[ki-idx]+=a[idx]
             in_peak[s:e+1]=True
       srcs = np.argsort(pressure)[-8:]
       if not in_peak.any(): in_peak = np.zeros(n,bool)
       candmask = ~in_peak
       if candmask.sum() < n//4: candmask = np.ones(n,bool)
       candmask[0]? Include all? If in_peak most, use all.
       cands=np.where(candmask)[0]
       # sort cands by low pressure
       cands = cands[np.argsort(pressure[cands])]
       cands = cands[:20]
       improved=False
       for src in srcs:
          src=int(src)
          if a[src] < 1e-12: continue
          for dst in cands:
             dst=int(dst)
             if dst==src: continue
             for frac in (0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.08):
                amt=a[src]*frac
                trial=a.copy(); trial[src]-=amt; trial[dst]+=amt
                trial=np.maximum(trial,0)
                ss=trial.sum()
                if ss<=0: continue
                trial=trial/ss
                cm=autocorr_fft(trial).max()
                if cm < best_c - 1e-15:
                   a=trial; best_c=cm; best_a=trial.copy(); no_improve=0; improved=True; break
             if improved: break
          if improved: break
       if not improved:
          # random tiny perturbation based on pressure: add small noise to low pressure
          if no_improve % 10 ==0:
             rng=...; i high pressure, j low pressure, frac random
             ...
    return best_a
```

def run_shape_search(n, time_limit, tag="shape"):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    B=make_basis(n)
    K=B.shape[1]
    beta=1000.0
    starts=[]
    starts.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(98765)
    starts.append(rng.uniform(-2,2,K))
    starts.append(rng.uniform(-1,1,K)*np.array([1,0.5,0.2,0.1,1,-1,0.5,0.5,0.2,-0.2,0.1,0.1])?)
    # maybe deterministic phased:
    s1=np.zeros(K); s1[4]=1.2; starts.append(s1)
    s2=np.zeros(K); s2[5]=-1.0; starts.append(s2)
    best_params=starts[0]; best_f=inf; best_a=norm_sum1(shape_to_a(best_params,n)); best_score=score_from_a(best_a)
    for idx,s0 in enumerate(starts):
       if time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_limit*0.9: break
       data={'B':B,'beta':beta,'n':n,'best_F':float('inf'),'best_x':None}
       maxiter = int(min(80, max(20, 10 + time_limit/2.0)))
       options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-9,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':20,'m':8,'disp':False}
       bounds=[(-4.0,4.0)]*K
       if _HAS_SCIPY:
           try:
              res=_scipy_optimize.minimize(shape_b_fun, s0.copy(), jac=shape_b_jac, args=(data,), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
           except Exception:
              res=None
       else: res=None
       px = data['best_x'] if data['best_x'] is not None else (res.x if res is not None else s0)
       px=np.asarray(px, dtype=np.float64); if len mismatch, px=s0
       a=shape_to_a(px,n)
       sc=score_from_a(a)
       if finite and sc<best_score: best...
       # also evaluate best_F? Use score.
    return best_params, best_a, best_score
```

```
def run_full_lbfgs(a_init,n,time_limit):
    a=norm_sum1(a_init)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_from_a(a)
    if not _HAS_SCIPY: return best_a
    t0=time.monotonic()
    x_cur=a_to_x(a)
    stages = selected based on time_limit
    for beta,iters in stages:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       data={'n':n,'beta':float(beta),'best_F':inf,'best_x':None}
       options={'maxiter':iters,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':20,'m':10,'disp':False}
       try:
          res=_scipy_optimize.minimize(full_b_fun, x_cur.copy(), jac=full_b_jac, args=(data,), method='L-BFGS-B', options=options)
       except Exception:
          res=None
       x_best=data['best_x'] if data['best_x'] is not None else (res.x if res is not None else x_cur)
       x_best=np.asarray(x_best,dtype=np.float64)
       if x_best.shape[0]!=n-1: x_best=x_cur
       cand=reconstruct_a(x_best,n)
       # sym
       sym=norm_sum1(0.5*(cand+cand[::-1]))
       sc=score_from_a(cand); sc_sym=score_from_a(sym)
       a_use=cand; sc_use=sc
       if sc_sym < sc_use: a_use=sym; sc_use=sc_sym
       if finite and sc_use<best_score:
           best_a=a_use.copy(); best_score=sc_use
           print(...)
       x_cur=a_to_x(a_use) # use best actual for next
    return best_a
```

`random_profile(n, seed)`:
```
def random_smooth_profile(n, seed, verbose=False):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    base=np.ones(n)
    for m in range(1,4):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.2,1.2)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       base += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + phase)
    base=np.maximum(base,0.0)
    s=base.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return norm_sum1(x**2)
    return base/s
```
This shape positive. Could produce high endpoint? okay.
Maybe include `parent + noise`: if parent exists, p = parent_N * exp(noise small); normalize.

   sc=score_from_a(a)
   if not np.isfinite(sc): return
   # skip duplicate: if any same len and allclose? expensive; skip.
   pool.append((sc, a.copy(), label))
```

```

Detailed code snippets:

```
def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`autocorr_fft`:
```
def autocorr_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.zeros(0)
    m = 2*n - 1
    size = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)
    return c[:m]
```

`conv_weights_a`:
```
def conv_weights_a(w, a):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = w.size
    if L == 0 or n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    out_len = L + n - 1
    size = next_pow2(out_len)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(np.ascontiguousarray(a[::-1]), size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(W * Ar, size)
    return out[n-1:n-1+n]
```

`smooth_lse`:
```
def smooth_lse(a, beta):
    c = autocorr_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0: return 0.0, c, c
    offset = float(c.max())
    best_idx = int(np.argmax(c))
    if beta <= 0:
       p = np.zeros_like(c); p[best_idx]=1.0; return offset,p,c
    shifted = beta * (c - offset)
    np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       p = np.zeros_like(c); p[best_idx]=1.0
       f0 = offset
    else:
       p = e / s
       f0 = offset + (1.0/beta)*float(np.log(s))
    return f0, p, c
```

`score_from_a`:
```
def score_from_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15: return inf
    a=a/s
    c=autocorr_fft(a)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0: m=0.0
    return float(2.0*a.size*m)
```

`norm_sum1`, `interpolate`, etc.

In `make_basis`, use `cols = []`, append arrays, `B = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64, copy=False)`.

`shape_to_a`:
```
def shape_to_a(params, n, B=None):
    params=np.asarray(params,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if B is None: B=make_basis(n)
    l = B.dot(params)
    z = l - np.max(l)
    p = np.exp(z)
    ss=float(p.sum())
    if ss <= 0: return np.ones(n)/n
    p = p / ss
    return p
```
Exp with l maybe large; subtract max. If l has nan? params bounded; if not, use nan_to_num.

`run_shape_search` uses `time_limit`. If `_HAS_SCIPY` false, still evaluate starts.
Need set `data['scale']`? no.

def full_b_fun(x, data):
    n=data['n']; beta=data['beta']; scale=data.get('scale',1.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e300
    y=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[0]=0.0; y[1:]=x
    z=y - float(np.max(y))
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z, -745, 0)) # z max 0, min maybe -inf
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300: return 1e300
    a=e/s
    f0,pw,c=smooth_lse(a, beta)
    F=float(scale)*float(n)*float(f0)
    if np.isfinite(F) and F < data.get('best_F', float('inf')):

    return F if np.isfinite(F) else 1e300

```python
"""
Search strategy:
- Optimize the scale-invariant profile u = n*a on the simplex sum(u)=n.
- Use low-dimensional Fourier/polynomial shape search to avoid the local minimum
  found by purely coordinate-local methods.
- Refine the full-length sequence with softmax + limited-memory BFGS on a
  log-sum-exp smoothed autocorrelation-maximum objective.
- Interpolate to larger lengths where discretization error usually decreases.
- Finish with a small peak-pressure local move and one final evaluator check.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_optimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def autocorr_fft(a):
    """Linear autocorrelation a*a via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)
    return c[:m]


def conv_weights_a(w, a):
    """
    Returns vector h of length n where
    h[j] = sum_k w[k] * a[k-j], zero-extending a.
    This is needed for gradients of P(z)^2.
    """
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = w.size
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = L + n - 1
    size = next_pow2(out_len)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(np.ascontiguousarray(a[::-1]), size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(W * Ar, size)
    return out[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def smooth_lse(a, beta):
    """
    Smoothed log-sum-exp objective for probability vector a (sum 1).
    Returns f0, lag weights p, and coefficients c.
    """
    c = autocorr_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, c, c
    offset = float(np.max(c))
    best_idx = int(np.argmax(c))
    if beta <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        p[best_idx] = 1.0
        return offset, p, c
    shifted = beta * (c - offset)
    np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0, out=shifted)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        p[best_idx] = 1.0
        f0 = offset
    else:
        p = e / s
        f0 = offset + (1.0 / beta) * float(np.log(s))
    return f0, p, c


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def simplex_project(z):
    """Project onto {x >= 0, sum x = 1}."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z
    u = np.sort(z)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - 1.0)
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if len(rho) == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    rho = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    return np.maximum(z - theta, 0.0)


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    """Scale-invariant interpolation of a normalized sequence to new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, int(new_n))
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    a = a / s
    if a.size == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    ss = float(out.sum())
    if ss < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return out / ss


def score_from_a(a):
    """FFT score matching evaluator for nonnegative normalized a."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = autocorr_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def make_basis(n):
    """
    Low-dimensional nonconstant basis:
    centered polynomials + first 4 Fourier harmonics.
    """
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    xm = x - 0.5
    cols = [
        xm,
        xm * xm,
        xm * xm * xm,
        xm * xm * xm * xm,
    ]
    for m in range(1, 5):
        th = 2.0 * np.pi * m * x
        cols.append(np.cos(th))
        cols.append(np.sin(th))
    B = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    return B


def shape_to_a(params, n, B=None):
    """Construct positive profile from log-profile parameters."""
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if B is None:
        B = make_basis(n)
    if params.size < B.shape[1]:
        tmp = np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:params.size] = params
        params = tmp
    else:
        params = params[:B.shape[1]]
    l = B.dot(params)
    l = np.nan_to_num(l, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    z = l - float(np.max(l))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
    ss = float(p.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return p / ss


def shape_b_fun(x, data):
    """Objective and cached best for low-dimensional shape search."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e300
    B = data["B"]
    beta = float(data["beta"])
    n = int(data["n"])
    l = B.dot(x)
    l = np.nan_to_num(l, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    z = l - float(np.max(l))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
    ss = float(p.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300:
        return 1e300
    a = p / ss
    f0, pw, c = smooth_lse(a, beta)
    F = float(n) * float(f0)
    if np.isfinite(F) and F < data.get("best_F", float("inf")):
        data["best_F"] = float(F)
        data["best_x"] = x.copy()
    return float(F) if np.isfinite(F) else 1e300


def shape_b_jac(x, data):
    """Gradient for low-dimensional shape search."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(data["n"])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64) if "B" in data else np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    B = data["B"]
    beta = float(data["beta"])
    l = B.dot(x)
    l = np.nan_to_num(l, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    z = l - float(np.max(l))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
    ss = float(p.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = p / ss
    f0, pw, c = smooth_lse(a, beta)
    g = (2.0 / beta) * conv_weights_a(pw, a)
    # dF/dx_j = sum_i p_i g_i T_{ij} - (sum_i p_i T_{ij}) (sum_i p_i g_i)
    BTa = B.T * a
    means = np.sum(BTa, axis=1)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, g))
    grad = BTa.dot(g) - means * dot
    return grad


def a_to_x(a):
    """Parameterize softmax with coefficient 0 anchored at zero."""
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    n = a.size
    floor = 1e-15 / max(1, n) + 1e-300
    y = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    y = y - float(y[0])
    y = np.clip(y, -35.0, 35.0)
    return y[1:].astype(np.float64, copy=True)


def reconstruct_a(x, n):
    """Reconstruct probability vector from anchored softmax parameters."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[0] = 0.0
    if x.size >= n - 1:
        y[1:] = x[:n - 1]
    else:
        y[1:x.size + 1] = x
        y[x.size + 1:] = -35.0
    z = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
    ss = float(e.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return e / ss


def full_b_fun(x, data):
    """Full-length softmax objective callback."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e300
    n = int(data["n"])
    beta = float(data["beta"])
    scale = float(data.get("scale", 1.0))
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[0] = 0.0
    if x.size >= n - 1:
        y[1:] = x[:n - 1]
    else:
        y[1:x.size + 1] = x
        y[x.size + 1:] = -35.0
    z = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
    ss = float(e.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300:
        return 1e300
    a = e / ss
    f0, pw, c = smooth_lse(a, beta)
    F = scale * float(n) * float(f0)
    if np.isfinite(F) and F < data.get("best_F", float("inf")):
        data["best_F"] = float(F)
        data["best_x"] = x[:n - 1].copy()
    return float(F) if np.isfinite(F) else 1e300


def full_b_jac(x, data):
    """Full-length softmax gradient callback."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(data["n"])
    out_len = max(0, n - 1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(out_len, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(data["beta"])
    scale = float(data.get("scale", 1.0))
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[0] = 0.0
    if x.size >= n - 1:
        y[1:] = x[:n - 1]
    else:
        y[1:x.size + 1] = x
        y[x.size + 1:] = -35.0
    z = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0))
    ss = float(e.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(out_len, dtype=np.float64)
    a = e / ss
    f0, pw, c = smooth_lse(a, beta)
    g = (2.0 * float(n) / beta) * scale * conv_weights_a(pw, a)
    total = float(np.dot(a, g))
    grad_y = a * (g - total * a)
    return grad_y[1:].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def default_initial(n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    a = (1.0 - x) ** 1.5 + 0.08 + 0.10 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    return norm_sum1(a)


def random_smooth_profile(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 4):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.2))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        base += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase)
        amp2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.5))
        phase2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        base += amp2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase2)
    return norm_sum1(base)


def consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a, label, t0):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    sc = score_from_a(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best_seq, best_score
    if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_seq = a.copy()
        best_score = sc
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        print("*** NEW BEST [" + str(label) + "]: " + repr(sc) +
              " (n=" + str(int(a.size)) + ", t=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s)")
    return best_seq, best_score


def _pool_sort_key(item):
    return item[0]


def add_pool(pool, a, label):
    if a is None:
        return
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    sc = score_from_a(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    pool.append((float(sc), a.copy(), str(label)))


def run_shape_search(n, time_limit):
    """Low-dimensional shape search at full target length."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    n = int(n)
    B = make_basis(n)
    K = B.shape[1]
    beta = 1000.0

    starts = []
    starts.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))

    s = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    s[4] = 1.2
    starts.append(s)

    s = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    s[5] = -1.2
    starts.append(s)

    s = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    s[1] = 0.8
    s[4] = -0.6
    s[7] = 0.4
    starts.append(s)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(13579)
    for _ in range(2):
        starts.append(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0, K).astype(np.float64))

    best_params = starts[0].copy()
    best_a = shape_to_a(best_params, n, B)
    best_score = score_from_a(best_a)

    for si, s0 in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(5.0, 0.85 * time_limit):
            break
        a0 = shape_to_a(s0, n, B)
        sc0 = score_from_a(a0)
        if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score:
            best_score = sc0
            best_params = s0.copy()
            best_a = a0.copy()

        if not _HAS_SCIPY:
            continue

        data = {"B": B, "beta": beta, "n": n, "best_F": float("inf"), "best_x": None}
        maxiter = int(min(90, max(20, 10 + time_limit / 2.5)))
        options = {
            "maxiter": maxiter,
            "ftol": 1e-9,
            "gtol": 1e-7,
            "maxls": 20,
            "m": 8,
            "disp": False,
        }
        bounds = [(-5.0, 5.0)] * K
        try:
            res = _scipy_optimize.minimize(
                shape_b_fun,
                s0.copy(),
                jac=shape_b_jac,
                args=(data,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options=options,
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        px = data.get("best_x")
        if px is None:
            px = res.x if res is not None else s0
        px = np.asarray(px, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if px.size != K:
            px = s0
        a1 = shape_to_a(px, n, B)
        sc1 = score_from_a(a1)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_score = sc1
            best_params = px.copy()
            best_a = a1.copy()

    print("  Shape search: score=" + repr(best_score) +
          " (n=" + str(n) + ", t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s)")
    return best_params, best_a, best_score


def run_full_lbfgs(a_init, n, time_limit):
    """Full-variable softmax L-BFGS refinement."""
    a = norm_sum1(a_init)
    n = int(n)
    if a.size != n:
        a = interpolate_sequence(a, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score_from_a(a)

    if not _HAS_SCIPY:
        return best_a

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if time_limit > 650.0:
        stages = [(200.0, 35), (800.0, 45), (2500.0, 55), (8000.0, 55)]
    elif time_limit > 300.0:
        stages = [(300.0, 30), (1500.0, 40), (5000.0, 40), (12000.0, 40)]
    elif time_limit > 150.0:
        stages = [(800.0, 25), (3000.0, 30), (8000.0, 30)]
    else:
        stages = [(2000.0, 25), (8000.0, 25)]

    x_cur = a_to_x(a)

    for bi, (beta, iters) in enumerate(stages):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
            break
        data = {
            "n": n,
            "beta": float(beta),
            "scale": float(n),
            "best_F": float("inf"),
            "best_x": None,
        }
        options = {
            "maxiter": int(iters),
            "ftol": 1e-10,
            "gtol": 1e-8,
            "maxls": 20,
            "m": 10,
            "disp": False,
        }
        try:
            res = _scipy_optimize.minimize(
                full_b_fun,
                x_cur.copy(),
                jac=full_b_jac,
                args=(data,),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=options,
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        x_best = data.get("best_x")
        if x_best is None:
            x_best = res.x if res is not None else x_cur
        x_best = np.asarray(x_best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x_best.size != n - 1:
            x_best = x_cur

        cand = reconstruct_a(x_best, n)
        sym = norm_sum1(0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1]))
        sc_cand = score_from_a(cand)
        sc_sym = score_from_a(sym)
        if np.isfinite(sc_sym) and sc_sym < sc_cand:
            cand = sym
            sc_cand = sc_sym

        if np.isfinite(sc_cand) and sc_cand < best_score:
            best_score = sc_cand
            best_a = cand.copy()
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print("    LBFGS stage " + str(bi + 1) + " beta=" + str(int(beta)) +
                  " new score=" + repr(sc_cand) + " (t=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s)")

        # Use candidate as warm start if not clearly harmful.
        if np.isfinite(sc_cand) and sc_cand <= best_score * 1.05 + 1e-8:
            x_cur = a_to_x(cand)
        else:
            x_cur = a_to_x(best_a)

    return best_a


def local_peak_move(a0, time_limit):
    """Small peak-pressure local search."""
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_c = float(autocorr_fft(a).max())
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    no_improve = 0
    max_stall = 25
    rng = np.random.default_rng(31415)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and no_improve < max_stall:
        c = autocorr_fft(a)
        c_max = float(c.max())
        sym = norm_sum1(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))
        sym_c = float(autocorr_fft(sym).max())
        if sym_c < best_c - 1e-14:
            a = sym
            best_c = sym_c
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            continue
        if c_max < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c_max
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        top = np.argsort(c)[-12:]
        pressure = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        in_peak = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
        for k in top:
            ki = int(k)
            s = max(0, ki - n + 1)
            e = min(n - 1, ki)
            if s > e:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(s, e + 1)
            left = a[idx]
            right = a[ki - idx]
            pressure[idx] += right
            pressure[ki - idx] += left
            in_peak[s:e + 1] = True

        srcs = np.argsort(pressure)[-4:]
        if in_peak.sum() > 0.75 * n:
            in_peak[:] = False
        cand_mask = ~in_peak
        if int(cand_mask.sum()) < max(50, n // 20):
            cand_mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
        cands = np.where(cand_mask)[0]
        cands = cands[np.argsort(pressure[cands])]
        cands = cands[:8]

        improved = False
        trials = 0
        max_trials = 36
        for src in srcs:
            src = int(src)
            if a[src] < 1e-13:
                continue
            for dst in cands:
                dst = int(dst)
                if dst == src:
                    continue
                for frac in (0.002, 0.01, 0.03):
                    trials += 1
                    if trials > max_trials or time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
                        break
                    amt = a[src] * frac
                    trial = a.copy()
                    trial[src] -= amt
                    trial[dst] += amt
                    trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                    ss = float(trial.sum())
                    if ss <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    trial = trial / ss
                    cm = float(autocorr_fft(trial).max())
                    if cm < best_c - 1e-15:
                        a = trial
                        best_c = cm
                        best_a = trial.copy()
                        no_improve = 0
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved or trials > max_trials:
                    break
            if improved or trials > max_trials:
                break
        if not improved and no_improve > 6:
            for _ in range(3):
                src = int(srcs[0])
                if a[src] < 1e-13:
                    break
                low = np.where(cand_mask)[0]
                if low.size == 0:
                    break
                dst = int(low[rng.integers(low.size)])
                if dst == src:
                    continue
                amt = a[src] * float(rng.uniform(0.001, 0.01))
                trial = a.copy()
                trial[src] -= amt
                trial[dst] += amt
                trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                ss = float(trial.sum())
                if ss <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                trial = trial / ss
                cm = float(autocorr_fft(trial).max())
                if cm < best_c - 1e-15:
                    a = trial
                    best_c = cm
                    best_a = trial.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    break
    return best_a


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    run_budget = max(300.0, min(total_budget - 90.0, 1710.0))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.isfinite(p).all():
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = norm_sum1(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, parent, "parent", t0)
        p_len = int(parent.size)
    else:
        p_len = 0

    target_main = 10000
    if p_len > target_main and p_len <= 13000:
        target_main = p_len
    N_MAIN = int(max(6000, min(target_main, 14000)))

    if best_seq is None:
        a = default_initial(N_MAIN)
        best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a, "default", t0)
    else:
        aN = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, N_MAIN)
        best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, aN, "parent_N", t0)
        parent_N = aN
    if parent is None:
        parent_N = default_initial(N_MAIN)

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    remaining = run_budget - elapsed
    shape_params = None
    if remaining > 220.0:
        shape_time = min(190.0, remaining * 0.22)
        print("\n=== Low-dim shape search (n=" + str(N_MAIN) + ") ===")
        shape_params, shape_a, _ = run_shape_search(N_MAIN, shape_time)
        best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, shape_a, "shape", t0)

    pool = []
    add_pool(pool, parent_N, "parent")
    if shape_params is not None:
        add_pool(pool, shape_to_a(shape_params, N_MAIN), "shape")
    if best_seq is not None:
        bN = best_seq if best_seq.size == N_MAIN else interpolate_sequence(best_seq, N_MAIN)
        add_pool(pool, bN, "best")
        add_pool(pool, bN[::-1], "best_rev")
        add_pool(pool, norm_sum1(0.5 * (bN + bN[::-1])), "best_sym")
    for seed in (17, 211, 3141):
        add_pool(pool, random_smooth_profile(N_MAIN, seed), "rand" + str(seed))

    pool.sort(key=_pool_sort_key)
    top = []
    seen_scores = []
    for sc, aa, lab in pool:
        if len(top) >= 4:
            break
        if any(abs(sc - s0) < 1e-9 for s0 in seen_scores):
            continue
        top.append((sc, aa, lab))
        seen_scores.append(sc)

    run_idx = 0
    for sc_init, a_init, label in top:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        remaining = run_budget - elapsed
        if remaining < 230.0:
            break
        if run_idx == 0:
            alloc = min(720.0, remaining * 0.55)
        elif run_idx == 1:
            alloc = min(420.0, remaining * 0.36)
        else:
            alloc = min(280.0, remaining * 0.30)
        if alloc < 120.0:
            break
        run_idx += 1
        print("\n=== Full LBFGS init=" + str(label) + " n=" + str(N_MAIN) +
              " alloc=" + str(round(alloc, 1)) + "s ===")
        a_opt = run_full_lbfgs(a_init, N_MAIN, alloc)
        best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_opt, "full_" + label, t0)

    if best_seq is not None:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        remaining = run_budget - elapsed
        cur_n = int(best_seq.size)

        print("\n=== Length extension ===")
        long_lengths = [12000, 14000, 16000, 18000, 20000]
        for L in long_lengths:
            if L <= cur_n or L > 20000:
                continue
            if remaining < 120.0:
                break
            aL = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, L)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, aL, "interp_" + str(L), t0)
            remaining = run_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)

        if best_seq.size < 20000:
            remaining = run_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
            if remaining > 260.0:
                N_long = min(20000, max(15000, int(round(int(best_seq.size) * 1.5 / 100.0) * 100)))
                if N_long > best_seq.size:
                    a_long = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, N_long)
                    best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_long, "long_" + str(N_long), t0)
                    alloc = min(320.0, remaining * 0.45)
                    if alloc > 80.0:
                        print("\n=== Full LBFGS long n=" + str(N_long) + " alloc=" + str(round(alloc, 1)) + "s ===")
                        a_opt = run_full_lbfgs(a_long, N_long, alloc)
                        best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_opt, "fulllong_" + str(N_long), t0)

        if best_seq.size < 20000:
            remaining = run_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
            if remaining > 180.0:
                a20 = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 20000)
                best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a20, "interp_20000", t0)
                if best_seq.size == 20000:
                    remaining = run_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
                    alloc = min(140.0, remaining * 0.40)
                    if alloc > 60.0:
                        print("\n=== Final 20000 LBFGS alloc=" + str(round(alloc, 1)) + "s ===")
                        a_opt = run_full_lbfgs(best_seq, 20000, alloc)
                        best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_opt, "full20000", t0)

        remaining = run_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if remaining > 120.0:
            loc_time = min(160.0, remaining * 0.35)
            print("\n=== Local peak polish alloc=" + str(round(loc_time, 1)) + "s ===")
            a_loc = local_peak_move(best_seq, loc_time)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_loc, "local", t0)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = default_initial(N_MAIN)
        best_score = score_from_a(best_seq)

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.nan_to_num(best_seq, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = np.minimum(best_seq, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        best_seq = default_initial(8000)
        s = 1.0
    else:
        best_seq = best_seq / s

    print("\nFINAL FFT estimate: score=" + repr(best_score) +
          ", n=" + str(int(best_seq.size)) +
          ", time=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s")

    try:
        real_score = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq])
        print("Final evaluator score: " + repr(real_score))
        if np.isfinite(real_score):
            best_score = min(best_score, real_score)
        else:
            small = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, 10000)
            real_score2 = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in small])
            if np.isfinite(real_score2):
                best_seq = small
                best_score = real_score2
    except Exception:
        pass

    print("PUBLISH n=" + str(int(best_seq.size)) + ", score=" + repr(best_score))
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
